  "content": "# Autonomous Repository Management Implementation Plan\n\nCreated: 2026-02-17\nStatus: PENDING\nApproved: No\nIterations: 0\nWorktree: Yes\n\n> **Status Lifecycle:** PENDING → COMPLETE → VERIFIED\n> **Iterations:** Tracks implement→verify cycles (incremented by verify phase)\n\n## Summary\n\n**Goal:** Implement end-to-end autonomous repository management so that /spec workflows and manual development flow autonomously from feature branch through PR creation, CI validation, and merge to main — with proper versioning, changelog, and release automation.\n\n**Architecture:** GitHub Flow (unchanged) with enforced quality gates via GitHub Rulesets, consolidated CI pipeline, squash-merge-only strategy, auto-merge on green CI, and release-please for automated versioning/changelog. The `/spec-verify` skill gains a new Step 3.12b that auto-creates PRs with auto-merge enabled after VERIFIED status.\n\n**Tech Stack:** GitHub Actions, GitHub Rulesets API, `gh` CLI, release-please-action v4, conventional commits (enforced via ruff + CI check)\n\n## Scope\n\n### In Scope\n\n- GitHub repo settings: squash-only merge, auto-merge, delete-branch-on-merge (via `gh repo edit`)\n- GitHub Ruleset on `main`: required status checks, no direct push, no force push\n- CI consolidation: merge 4 workflows into 2 (ci.yml + release.yml), remove `continue-on-error` from blocking checks\n- UV dependency caching in CI\n- release-please workflow for automated versioning + CHANGELOG.md\n- Conventional commit validation in CI (Python-based, no Node dependency)\n- Updated PR template with checklist\n- `/spec-verify` Step 3.12b: auto-create PR after VERIFIED, enable auto-merge\n- Setup script (`scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh`) for one-time repo configuration via `gh` CLI\n\n### Out of Scope\n\n- Dependabot/Renovate (separate concern, add later)\n- CodeQL/Trivy security scanning (separate concern)\n- Commit signing verification (requires GPG setup per-developer)\n- Merge queue (requires branch protection or rulesets to be active first — can enable after this lands)\n- GitHub Actions for auto-labeling or stale PR management\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- `gh` CLI authenticated with admin access (verified: `viewerPermission: ADMIN`)\n- GitHub Actions enabled on the repository (verified: workflows exist)\n- Existing CI scripts in `scripts/ci/` (verified: 4 scripts exist)\n\n## Context for Implementer\n\n- **Patterns to follow:** Existing CI workflows in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` for job structure. Pre-commit config in `.pre-commit-config.yaml` for hook patterns.\n- **Conventions:** Conventional commits (`feat:`, `fix:`, etc.) already documented in `.claude/rules/git-workflow.md` but not enforced in CI.\n- **Key files:**\n  - `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — main CI pipeline (keep, rewrite)\n  - `.github/workflows/test.yml` — duplicate of ci.yml test job (delete)\n  - `.github/workflows/lint.yml` — duplicate of ci.yml lint job (delete)\n  - `.github/workflows/repo-health.yml` — weekly health check (keep separate, it has a schedule trigger)\n  - `.github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md` — minimal, needs expansion\n  - `pyproject.toml` — version is `1.0.0`, release-please will manage it\n  - `.claude/commands/spec-verify.md` — Step 3.12 is where PR creation integrates\n- **Gotchas:**\n  - `tests/unit/` exists but ci.yml references it separately from `tests/` — the consolidated pipeline should run the full suite\n  - `continue-on-error: true` on lint/typecheck/compound jobs means they currently never block merges\n  - `cloud-vault-mcp/` is a separate sub-project with its own tests — keep it in CI\n  - release-please `python` type expects `setup.py` or `setup.cfg` — for pyproject.toml-only projects, use `simple` type with `extra-files` to bump version in pyproject.toml\n  - No PAT secret exists yet for release-please — `GITHUB_TOKEN` works but won't trigger downstream workflows\n\n## Runtime Environment\n\n- **Start command:** `uv run uvicorn cohezion.api:app --reload`\n- **Port:** 8080\n- **Health check:** `curl http://localhost:8080/health`\n- **CI is serverless:** GitHub Actions — no deploy step needed for CI changes\n\n## Progress Tracking\n\n**MANDATORY: Update this checklist as tasks complete.**\n\n- [ ] Task 1: Configure GitHub repo settings via gh CLI\n- [ ] Task 2: Consolidate CI workflows\n- [ ] Task 3: Add UV caching to CI\n- [ ] Task 4: Add conventional commit check to CI\n- [ ] Task 5: Add release-please workflow\n- [ ] Task 6: Create GitHub Ruleset on main\n- [ ] Task 7: Update PR template\n- [ ] Task 8: Add PR auto-creation to /spec-verify\n- [ ] Task 9: Create setup script for repo configuration\n\n**Total Tasks:** 9 | **Completed:** 0 | **Remaining:** 9\n\n## Implementation Tasks\n\n### Task 1: Configure GitHub Repo Settings\n\n**Objective:** Set squash-merge-only, enable auto-merge, enable delete-branch-on-merge via `gh repo edit`.\n\n**Dependencies:** None\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` (idempotent setup script)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Use `gh repo edit` with flags: `--enable-squash-merge --enable-merge-commit=false --enable-rebase-merge=false --enable-auto-merge --delete-branch-on-merge`\n- Script is idempotent — safe to re-run\n- This is a one-time setup but capturing in a script ensures reproducibility\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] `gh repo view --json squashMergeAllowed,mergeCommitAllowed,rebaseMergeAllowed,deleteBranchOnMerge` shows `{squashMergeAllowed:true, mergeCommitAllowed:false, rebaseMergeAllowed:false, deleteBranchOnMerge:true}`\n- [ ] `gh api repos/manderson240/cohezion --jq '.allow_auto_merge'` returns `true`\n\n**Verify:**\n- `bash scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` runs without error\n- `gh repo view manderson240/cohezion --json squashMergeAllowed,mergeCommitAllowed,rebaseMergeAllowed,deleteBranchOnMerge`\n\n---\n\n### Task 2: Consolidate CI Workflows\n\n**Objective:** Merge `ci.yml`, `test.yml`, and `lint.yml` into a single `ci.yml`. Remove `continue-on-error: true` from lint and validate jobs so they actually block merges. Keep `repo-health.yml` separate (has schedule trigger).\n\n**Dependencies:** None\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (rewrite)\n- Delete: `.github/workflows/test.yml`\n- Delete: `.github/workflows/lint.yml`\n- Modify: `.github/workflows/repo-health.yml` (minor: add uv caching — done in Task 3)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Jobs: `lint` (ruff format + ruff check — MUST pass), `validate` (agent/skill/registry validation — MUST pass), `test` (pytest on 3.11+3.13 matrix — MUST pass), `typecheck` (mypy — advisory, keep `continue-on-error`), `compound` (audit — advisory, keep `continue-on-error`)\n- `ci-status` summary job: only gates on lint + validate + test (the three required checks)\n- Cloud-vault-mcp tests stay in the pipeline (install + test as separate step in test job)\n- Trigger: `push` to `main` + `pull_request` to `main` + `merge_group` (for future merge queue)\n- Use `actions/setup-python@v5` (latest) and `astral-sh/setup-uv@v5` for proper uv caching\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] Only `ci.yml` and `repo-health.yml` exist in `.github/workflows/`\n- [ ] `lint` job fails if ruff finds errors (no `continue-on-error`)\n- [ ] `validate` job fails if agent/skill validation fails (no `continue-on-error`)\n- [ ] `test` job runs on Python 3.11 and 3.13 matrix\n- [ ] `ci-status` summary job gates on lint + validate + test\n- [ ] `merge_group` trigger is present for future merge queue support\n\n**Verify:**\n- `ls .github/workflows/` shows only `ci.yml`, `repo-health.yml`, `release.yml`\n- `grep -c 'continue-on-error' .github/workflows/ci.yml` — only in typecheck and compound jobs\n\n---\n\n### Task 3: Add UV Caching to CI\n\n**Objective:** Use `astral-sh/setup-uv@v5` action with built-in caching to speed up CI installs from ~30s to ~3s.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 2\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (replace `pip install uv` + `uv sync` with `astral-sh/setup-uv@v5`)\n- Modify: `.github/workflows/repo-health.yml` (no Python needed — pure bash, skip)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- `astral-sh/setup-uv@v5` includes built-in caching with `enable-cache: true`\n- Replaces manual `pip install uv` and `uv sync --frozen` steps\n- Cache key based on `uv.lock` file hash (automatic)\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] CI uses `astral-sh/setup-uv@v5` with `enable-cache: true`\n- [ ] No `pip install uv` commands remain in ci.yml\n- [ ] `uv sync --frozen` used for reproducible installs\n\n**Verify:**\n- `grep 'astral-sh/setup-uv' .github/workflows/ci.yml` returns matches\n- `grep 'pip install uv' .github/workflows/ci.yml` returns no matches\n\n---\n\n### Task 4: Add Conventional Commit Check to CI\n\n**Objective:** Validate that PR titles follow conventional commit format (`feat:`, `fix:`, `refactor:`, etc.) since GitHub squash-merge uses the PR title as the commit message.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 2\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `scripts/ci/check_pr_title.py` (pure Python, no Node dependencies)\n- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (add `commit-lint` job)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Since we're using squash merge, the PR TITLE becomes the commit message — validate that, not individual commits\n- Pure Python script using regex — no commitlint/Node dependency needed\n- Pattern: `^(feat|fix|refactor|test|docs|chore|perf|ci|build|style|revert)(\\(.+\\))?!?: .+`\n- Job only runs on `pull_request` events (not `push` or `merge_group`)\n- Uses `${{ github.event.pull_request.title }}` as input\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] `scripts/ci/check_pr_title.py` validates conventional commit format\n- [ ] CI job `commit-lint` runs on PRs and fails if title doesn't match\n- [ ] Script accepts valid titles: `feat: add auth`, `fix(api): handle null`, `refactor!: break API`\n- [ ] Script rejects invalid titles: `Add auth`, `fixed bug`, `WIP`\n\n**Verify:**\n- `echo \"feat: add feature\" | uv run python scripts/ci/check_pr_title.py` exits 0\n- `echo \"bad title\" | uv run python scripts/ci/check_pr_title.py` exits 1\n\n---\n\n### Task 5: Add release-please Workflow\n\n**Objective:** Add release-please GitHub Action that auto-creates release PRs with version bumps and CHANGELOG.md based on conventional commits merged to main.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 4 (conventional commits must be enforced first)\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `.github/workflows/release.yml`\n- Create: `release-please-config.json`\n- Create: `.release-please-manifest.json`\n- Create: `CHANGELOG.md` (empty initial file)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Use manifest config (advanced) for explicit control over Python versioning\n- `release-please-config.json`: release-type `simple` with `extra-files` pointing to `pyproject.toml` for version bump (the `python` type expects setup.py which we don't have)\n- `.release-please-manifest.json`: `{\".\": \"1.0.0\"}` (current version)\n- Use `secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN` initially (simpler setup, known limitation: release PRs won't trigger CI — acceptable for solo dev, upgrade to PAT later if needed)\n- Workflow triggers on `push` to `main` only\n- Outputs `release_created` and `tag_name` for potential post-release steps\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] `.github/workflows/release.yml` exists with release-please-action v4\n- [ ] `release-please-config.json` configured for Python with pyproject.toml version bump\n- [ ] `.release-please-manifest.json` has current version `1.0.0`\n- [ ] `CHANGELOG.md` exists (empty or with initial entry)\n\n**Verify:**\n- `cat release-please-config.json | python -m json.tool` — valid JSON\n- `cat .release-please-manifest.json | python -m json.tool` — valid JSON with version\n- `ls .github/workflows/release.yml` — file exists\n\n---\n\n### Task 6: Create GitHub Ruleset on Main\n\n**Objective:** Create a GitHub Ruleset that protects `main` with required status checks, no direct push, no force push, and no branch deletion.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 2 (CI job names must be finalized first so we reference the correct status checks)\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` (add ruleset creation via `gh api`)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Use GitHub Rulesets API (modern replacement for branch protection rules, recommended as of 2025)\n- Ruleset name: `main-protection`\n- Rules: `pull_request` (require PR before merge), `required_status_checks` (lint, validate, test, ci-status), `non_fast_forward` (no force push), `deletion` (no branch deletion)\n- Bypass actors: repository admin (owner) — allows emergency direct pushes\n- Created via `gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/rulesets --method POST` with JSON body\n- Script checks if ruleset already exists before creating (idempotent)\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] `gh api repos/manderson240/cohezion/rulesets` returns a ruleset named `main-protection`\n- [ ] Ruleset requires `lint`, `validate`, `test`, `ci-status` status checks\n- [ ] Ruleset blocks direct push to main (requires PR)\n- [ ] Ruleset blocks force push to main\n- [ ] Admin bypass is configured for emergency use\n\n**Verify:**\n- `gh api repos/manderson240/cohezion/rulesets --jq '.[].name'` returns `main-protection`\n- `bash scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` is idempotent (running twice doesn't error)\n\n---\n\n### Task 7: Update PR Template\n\n**Objective:** Replace the minimal PR template with a comprehensive checklist that matches the project's workflow.\n\n**Dependencies:** None\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `.github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md`\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Sections: Summary (auto-filled by /spec), Change Type (dropdown), Testing (checklist), Checklist (pre-merge verification)\n- Include conventional commit reminder for PR title\n- Reference plan file if applicable\n- Keep it concise — don't over-template\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] PR template has Summary, Change Type, Testing, and Checklist sections\n- [ ] Template includes conventional commit format reminder\n- [ ] Template is under 40 lines\n\n**Verify:**\n- `wc -l .github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md` — under 40 lines\n- `cat .github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md` — readable and complete\n\n---\n\n### Task 8: Add PR Auto-Creation to /spec-verify\n\n**Objective:** After `/spec-verify` sets plan status to VERIFIED and worktree is synced, automatically create a PR with auto-merge enabled. This is a new Step 3.12b in the spec-verify skill.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 1 (auto-merge must be enabled), Task 6 (rulesets must exist for auto-merge to work)\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `~/.claude/commands/spec-verify.md` (add Step 3.12b after Step 3.12)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- New step 3.12b runs AFTER status is set to VERIFIED and worktree sync completes\n- Uses `gh pr create` with `--title` (conventional commit format from plan name) and `--body` (auto-generated from plan summary + task list)\n- Then `gh pr merge --auto --squash` to enable auto-merge\n- Only runs if worktree sync created a commit on a feature branch (not if already on main)\n- PR title format: `feat: <plan-slug-as-readable-title>` (derived from plan filename)\n- PR body: plan Summary section + completed tasks list\n- Skip if PR already exists for the branch (idempotent)\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] spec-verify.md has Step 3.12b that creates PR after VERIFIED status\n- [ ] PR is created with conventional commit title\n- [ ] Auto-merge is enabled via `gh pr merge --auto --squash`\n- [ ] Step is skipped if no feature branch or PR already exists\n\n**Verify:**\n- `grep '3.12b' ~/.claude/commands/spec-verify.md` — step exists\n- `grep 'gh pr create' ~/.claude/commands/spec-verify.md` — PR creation command present\n- `grep 'gh pr merge --auto' ~/.claude/commands/spec-verify.md` — auto-merge command present\n\n---\n\n### Task 9: Create Repo Setup Script\n\n**Objective:** Consolidate all one-time repo configuration into a single idempotent setup script that can be run to bootstrap or verify repo settings.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 1, Task 6\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` (finalize with all configuration)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Script combines: repo settings (Task 1) + ruleset creation (Task 6)\n- Idempotent: checks current state before making changes\n- Includes verification output at the end (shows current settings)\n- Can be run by any admin to verify/restore repo configuration\n- Add to `Makefile` as `repo-setup` target\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] `scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` configures all repo settings and rulesets\n- [ ] Script is idempotent (safe to re-run)\n- [ ] Script outputs verification summary\n- [ ] `make repo-setup` target exists in Makefile\n\n**Verify:**\n- `bash scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` — completes without error\n- `make repo-setup` — runs the setup script\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n- **Unit tests:** `scripts/ci/check_pr_title.py` — test with valid/invalid PR title inputs\n- **Integration tests:** Push the CI changes to a branch, create a test PR, verify CI runs and checks pass\n- **Manual verification:**\n  1. Run `scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` and verify repo settings via `gh repo view`\n  2. Create a PR with a non-conventional title, verify CI fails\n  3. Create a PR with a conventional title, verify CI passes\n  4. After merge to main, verify release-please creates a release PR\n\n## Risks and Mitigations\n\n| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |\n|------|-----------|--------|------------|\n| Existing PRs break with squash-only | Low | Medium | No open PRs currently; apply after merging current `spec/routes-consolidation` branch |\n| CI job names change and break ruleset | Medium | Medium | Ruleset references explicit job names from consolidated ci.yml; document the mapping in setup script comments |\n| release-please GITHUB_TOKEN can't trigger CI on release PRs | High | Low | Acceptable for solo dev — release PRs are auto-merged by release-please itself. Document upgrade path to PAT if needed |\n| Removing continue-on-error reveals existing lint/type errors | High | Low | Known: mypy and compound audit have existing issues. Keep `continue-on-error` on those two advisory jobs only |\n\n## Open Questions\n\n- None — task description was comprehensive and exploration confirmed feasibility.\n\n### Deferred Ideas\n\n- **Merge queue:** Enable after rulesets are in place and CI is stable. Requires `merge_group` trigger (added in Task 2) but ruleset config is separate.\n- **Dependabot/Renovate:** Automated dependency updates — separate concern, add after core pipeline is stable.\n- **CodeQL scanning:** Security analysis — add as an advisory CI job in a future iteration.\n- **PAT for release-please:** If downstream workflow triggers are needed, create a PAT and store as repo secret.\n"
}

   118→**Objective:** Merge `ci.yml`, `test.yml`, and `lint.yml` into a single `ci.yml`. Remove `continue-on-error: true` from lint and validate jobs so they actually block merges. Keep `repo-health.yml` separate (has schedule trigger).
   119→
   120→**Dependencies:** None
   121→
   122→**Files:**
   123→- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (rewrite)
   124→- Delete: `.github/workflows/test.yml`
   125→- Delete: `.github/workflows/lint.yml`
   126→- Modify: `.github/workflows/repo-health.yml` (minor: add uv caching — done in Task 3)
   127→
   128→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   129→- Jobs: `lint` (ruff format + ruff check — MUST pass), `validate` (agent/skill/registry validation — MUST pass), `test` (pytest on 3.11+3.13 matrix — MUST pass), `typecheck` (mypy — advisory, keep `continue-on-error`), `compound` (audit — advisory, keep `continue-on-error`)
   130→- `ci-status` summary job: only gates on lint + validate + test (the three required checks)
   131→- Cloud-vault-mcp tests stay in the pipeline (install + test as separate step in test job)
   132→- Trigger: `push` to `main` + `pull_request` to `main` + `merge_group` (for future merge queue)
   133→- Use `actions/setup-python@v5` (latest) and `astral-sh/setup-uv@v5` for proper uv caching
   134→
   135→**Definition of Done:**
   136→- [ ] Only `ci.yml` and `repo-health.yml` exist in `.github/workflows/`
   137→- [ ] `lint` job fails if ruff finds errors (no `continue-on-error`)
   138→- [ ] `validate` job fails if agent/skill validation fails (no `continue-on-error`)
   139→- [ ] `test` job runs on Python 3.11 and 3.13 matrix
   140→- [ ] `ci-status` summary job gates on lint + validate + test
   141→- [ ] `merge_group` trigger is present for future merge queue support
   142→
   143→**Verify:**
   144→- `ls .github/workflows/` shows only `ci.yml`, `repo-health.yml`, `release.yml`
   145→- `grep -c 'continue-on-error' .github/workflows/ci.yml` — only in typecheck and compound jobs
   146→
   147→---
   148→
   149→### Task 3: Add UV Caching to CI
   150→
   151→**Objective:** Use `astral-sh/setup-uv@v5` action with built-in caching to speed up CI installs from ~30s to ~3s.
   152→
   153→**Dependencies:** Task 2
   154→
   155→**Files:**
   156→- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (replace `pip install uv` + `uv sync` with `astral-sh/setup-uv@v5`)
   157→- Modify: `.github/workflows/repo-health.yml` (no Python needed — pure bash, skip)
   158→
   159→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   160→- `astral-sh/setup-uv@v5` includes built-in caching with `enable-cache: true`
   161→- Replaces manual `pip install uv` and `uv sync --frozen` steps
   162→- Cache key based on `uv.lock` file hash (automatic)
   163→
   164→**Definition of Done:**
   165→- [ ] CI uses `astral-sh/setup-uv@v5` with `enable-cache: true`
   166→- [ ] No `pip install uv` commands remain in ci.yml
   167→- [ ] `uv sync --frozen` used for reproducible installs
   168→
   169→**Verify:**
   170→- `grep 'astral-sh/setup-uv' .github/workflows/ci.yml` returns matches
   171→- `grep 'pip install uv' .github/workflows/ci.yml` returns no matches
   172→
   173→---
   174→
   175→### Task 4: Add Conventional Commit Check to CI
   176→
   177→**Objective:** Validate that PR titles follow conventional commit format (`feat:`, `fix:`, `refactor:`, etc.) since GitHub squash-merge uses the PR title as the commit message.
   178→
   179→**Dependencies:** Task 2
   180→
   181→**Files:**
   182→- Create: `scripts/ci/check_pr_title.py` (pure Python, no Node dependencies)
   183→- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (add `commit-lint` job)
   184→
   185→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   186→- Since we're using squash merge, the PR TITLE becomes the commit message — validate that, not individual commits
   187→- Pure Python script using regex — no commitlint/Node dependency needed
   188→- Pattern: `^(feat|fix|refactor|test|docs|chore|perf|ci|build|style|revert)(\(.+\))?!?: .+`
   189→- Job only runs on `pull_request` events (not `push` or `merge_group`)
   190→- Uses `${{ github.event.pull_request.title }}` as input
   191→
   192→**Definition of Done:**
   193→- [ ] `scripts/ci/check_pr_title.py` validates conventional commit format
   194→- [ ] CI job `commit-lint` runs on PRs and fails if title doesn't match
   195→- [ ] Script accepts valid titles: `feat: add auth`, `fix(api): handle null`, `refactor!: break API`
   196→- [ ] Script rejects invalid titles: `Add auth`, `fixed bug`, `WIP`
   197→
   198→**Verify:**
   199→- `echo "feat: add feature" | uv run python scripts/ci/check_pr_title.py` exits 0
   200→- `echo "bad title" | uv run python scripts/ci/check_pr_title.py` exits 1
   201→
   202→---
   203→
   204→### Task 5: Add release-please Workflow
   205→
   206→**Objective:** Add release-please GitHub Action that auto-creates release PRs with version bumps and CHANGELOG.md based on conventional commits merged to main.
   207→
   208→**Dependencies:** Task 4 (conventional commits must be enforced first)
   209→
   210→**Files:**
   211→- Create: `.github/workflows/release.yml`
   212→- Create: `release-please-config.json`
   213→- Create: `.release-please-manifest.json`
   214→- Create: `CHANGELOG.md` (empty initial file)
   215→
   216→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   217→- Use manifest config (advanced) for explicit control over Python versioning
   218→- `release-please-config.json`: release-type `simple` with `extra-files` pointing to `pyproject.toml` for version bump (the `python` type expects setup.py which we don't have)
   219→- `.release-please-manifest.json`: `{".": "1.0.0"}` (current version)
   220→- Use `secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN` initially (simpler setup, known limitation: release PRs won't trigger CI — acceptable for solo dev, upgrade to PAT later if needed)
   221→- Workflow triggers on `push` to `main` only
   222→- Outputs `release_created` and `tag_name` for potential post-release steps
   223→
   224→**Definition of Done:**
   225→- [ ] `.github/workflows/release.yml` exists with release-please-action v4
   226→- [ ] `release-please-config.json` configured for Python with pyproject.toml version bump
   227→- [ ] `.release-please-manifest.json` has current version `1.0.0`
   228→- [ ] `CHANGELOG.md` exists (empty or with initial entry)
   229→
   230→**Verify:**
   231→- `cat release-please-config.json | python -m json.tool` — valid JSON
   232→- `cat .release-please-manifest.json | python -m json.tool` — valid JSON with version
   233→- `ls .github/workflows/release.yml` — file exists
   234→
   235→---
   236→
   237→### Task 6: Create GitHub Ruleset on Main
   238→
   239→**Objective:** Create a GitHub Ruleset that protects `main` with required status checks, no direct push, no force push, and no branch deletion.
   240→
   241→**Dependencies:** Task 2 (CI job names must be finalized first so we reference the correct status checks)
   242→
   243→**Files:**
   244→- Modify: `scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` (add ruleset creation via `gh api`)
   245→
   246→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   247→- Use GitHub Rulesets API (modern replacement for branch protection rules, recommended as of 2025)
   248→- Ruleset name: `main-protection`
   249→- Rules: `pull_request` (require PR before merge), `required_status_checks` (lint, validate, test, ci-status), `non_fast_forward` (no force push), `deletion` (no branch deletion)
   250→- Bypass actors: repository admin (owner) — allows emergency direct pushes
   251→- Created via `gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/rulesets --method POST` with JSON body
   252→- Script checks if ruleset already exists before creating (idempotent)
   253→
   254→**Definition of Done:**
   255→- [ ] `gh api repos/manderson240/cohezion/rulesets` returns a ruleset named `main-protection`
   256→- [ ] Ruleset requires `lint`, `validate`, `test`, `ci-status` status checks
   257→- [ ] Ruleset blocks direct push to main (requires PR)
   258→- [ ] Ruleset blocks force push to main
   259→- [ ] Admin bypass is configured for emergency use
   260→
   261→**Verify:**
   262→- `gh api repos/manderson240/cohezion/rulesets --jq '.[].name'` returns `main-protection`
   263→- `bash scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` is idempotent (running twice doesn't error)
   264→
   265→---

   292→
   293→### Task 8: Add PR Auto-Creation to /spec-verify
   294→
   295→**Objective:** After `/spec-verify` sets plan status to VERIFIED and worktree is synced, automatically create a PR with auto-merge enabled. This is a new Step 3.12b in the spec-verify skill.
   296→
   297→**Dependencies:** Task 1 (auto-merge must be enabled), Task 6 (rulesets must exist for auto-merge to work)
   298→
   299→**Files:**
   300→- Modify: `~/.claude/commands/spec-verify.md` (add Step 3.12b after Step 3.12)
   301→
   302→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   303→- New step 3.12b runs AFTER status is set to VERIFIED and worktree sync completes
   304→- Uses `gh pr create` with `--title` (conventional commit format from plan name) and `--body` (auto-generated from plan summary + task list)
   305→- Then `gh pr merge --auto --squash` to enable auto-merge
   306→- Only runs if worktree sync created a commit on a feature branch (not if already on main)
   307→- PR title format: `feat: <plan-slug-as-readable-title>` (derived from plan filename)
   308→- PR body: plan Summary section + completed tasks list
   309→- Skip if PR already exists for the branch (idempotent)
   310→
   311→**Definition of Done:**

   313→- [ ] PR is created with conventional commit title
   314→- [ ] Auto-merge is enabled via `gh pr merge --auto --squash`
   315→- [ ] Step is skipped if no feature branch or PR already exists
   316→

   319→- `grep 'gh pr create' ~/.claude/commands/spec-verify.md` — PR creation command present
   320→- `grep 'gh pr merge --auto' ~/.claude/commands/spec-verify.md` — auto-merge command present

   323→
   324→### Task 9: Create Repo Setup Script
   325→
   326→**Objective:** Consolidate all one-time repo configuration into a single idempotent setup script that can be run to bootstrap or verify repo settings.
   327→
   328→**Dependencies:** Task 1, Task 6
   329→
   330→**Files:**
   331→- Modify: `scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` (finalize with all configuration)
   332→
   333→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   334→- Script combines: repo settings (Task 1) + ruleset creation (Task 6)
   335→- Idempotent: checks current state before making changes
   336→- Includes verification output at the end (shows current settings)
   337→- Can be run by any admin to verify/restore repo configuration
   338→- Add to `Makefile` as `repo-setup` target
   339→
   340→**Definition of Done:**
   341→- [ ] `scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` configures all repo settings and rulesets
   342→- [ ] Script is idempotent (safe to re-run)
   343→- [ ] Script outputs verification summary
   344→- [ ] `make repo-setup` target exists in Makefile
   345→
   346→**Verify:**
   347→- `bash scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` — completes without error
   348→- `make repo-setup` — runs the setup script
   349→
   350→## Testing Strategy
   351→
   352→- **Unit tests:** `scripts/ci/check_pr_title.py` — test with valid/invalid PR title inputs
   353→- **Integration tests:** Push the CI changes to a branch, create a test PR, verify CI runs and checks pass
   354→- **Manual verification:**
   355→  1. Run `scripts/ci/setup_repo.sh` and verify repo settings via `gh repo view`
   356→  2. Create a PR with a non-conventional title, verify CI fails
   357→  3. Create a PR with a conventional title, verify CI passes
   358→  4. After merge to main, verify release-please creates a release PR
   359→
   360→## Risks and Mitigations
   361→
   362→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
   363→|------|-----------|--------|------------|
   364→| Existing PRs break with squash-only | Low | Medium | No open PRs currently; apply after merging current `spec/routes-consolidation` branch |
   365→| CI job names change and break ruleset | Medium | Medium | Ruleset references explicit job names from consolidated ci.yml; document the mapping in setup script comments |
   366→| release-please GITHUB_TOKEN can't trigger CI on release PRs | High | Low | Acceptable for solo dev — release PRs are auto-merged by release-please itself. Document upgrade path to PAT if needed |
   367→| Removing continue-on-error reveals existing lint/type errors | High | Low | Known: mypy and compound audit have existing issues. Keep `continue-on-error` on those two advisory jobs only |
   368→
   369→## Open Questions
   370→
   371→- None — task description was comprehensive and exploration confirmed feasibility.
   372→
   373→### Deferred Ideas
   374→
   375→- **Merge queue:** Enable after rulesets are in place and CI is stable. Requires `merge_group` trigger (added in Task 2) but ruleset config is separate.
   376→- **Dependabot/Renovate:** Automated dependency updates — separate concern, add after core pipeline is stable.
   377→- **CodeQL scanning:** Security analysis — add as an advisory CI job in a future iteration.
   378→- **PAT for release-please:** If downstream workflow triggers are needed, create a PAT and store as repo secret.
   379→
